Let's design local search algorithm:
```
def improve_sequence(a, budget_s, rng, final_n=None):
    n=len(a); S=np.sqrt(2*n)
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000); normalize to S
    best=a; best_score=score_fft(best)
    active_top = maybe 5
    lr values [0.001,0.003,0.01,0.03,0.1]
    for iterations until time:
       C=fft_convolution(best)
       active = top active_lags(C, maybe 1+random)
       for lr in [0.002,0.008,0.02,0.06]:
          loga=log(best+1e-12)
          grad=zeros
          for k in active:
             # contributions c_i = best[i]*best[k-i] valid; grad += c_i
             # maybe derivative of C_k is 2c_i; scale by max
          grad /= max(grad max, C[k]?)
          loga -= lr*grad
          # optional add smoothed noise
          new=exp(loga); normalize S; clip
          new_score=score_fft(new)
          if improve: best=new; break
       # also random perturb, pair transfers, block multipliers
       # record best
```

Local search details robust:
```
def convolution_fft(a):
    n=len(a); nfft=1
    L=2*n-1
    while nfft<L: nfft<<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,n=nfft)[:L]
    # clean small negatives
    # return c, nfft
```
Use cache? In improve, keep C for best.

Score function:
```
def internal_score(a):
    c,_=convolution_fft(a)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=0: return inf
    return float(2*len(a)*c.max()/(S*S))
```
Normalization:
```
def normalize_sum(a, target_s):
   s=a.sum(); if s<=0: target uniform?; return a*(target_s/s)
```

`update_pair_transfer`:
For active k, compute contrib vector. Source index: choose i weighted by contrib (or high). Destination: choose j weighted by (small) base weight maybe a[j]*a[k-j]? def build_sequence_params(n,x):
   t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   logf = x[0]*(t-0.5) + x[1]*(t-0.5)**2 + x[2]*(t-0.5)**3
   # sinusoids: pairs
   for k in range(3):
      A=x[3+2*k]; B=x[4+2*k]
      angle=2*np.pi*k*(t)+? base zero at 0.5? maybe use centered: angle=2π k (t-0.5)
      logf += A*np.sin(angle)+B*np.cos(angle)
   # dynamic range clip
   logf -= logf.max() # to avoid overflow
   f=np.exp(logf)
   f=np.clip(f,0,1000)
   normalize
```

`normalize_to_sum(a, target_s=None)`:
```
def normalize_to_sum(a, target_s=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=np.sum(a)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-12:
       a=np.ones_like(a)
       s=a.size
    if target_s is None:
       target_s=np.sqrt(2.0*a.size)
    a = a * (target_s / s)
    return np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
```
If clipping after scaling to 1000 may reduce sum; okay maybe normalize again? We can loop once:
```
a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=sum; if s<target_s*0.9: scale again? But values likely <1000.
```
To ensure sum close target, scale after clip only if max not clipped. We can:
```
scale=target_s/s; a*=scale; if a.max()>1000: a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=np.sum(a); if s>0: a*=target_s/s
```
but second scale might exceed1000 if max already clipped. Our values small ~100/n? n>=1000, max likely <1000. Fine.

`fft_convolution(a)`:
```
def fft_convolution(a):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    nfft=1
    while nfft<L: nfft <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=nfft)[:L]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
Could optimize by precomputing nfft per n? Not needed.

`internal_score(a)`:
```
def internal_score(a):
    S=np.sum(a)
    if S <= 0.01? internal can return inf if S small
    c=fft_convolution(a)
    M=c.max()
    return float(2.0*len(a)*M/(S*S))
```
For optimized sum S~√(2n) >1, okay.

def param_objective(x, n):

    s=internal_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(s): return 1e9
    return s

`build_exp_model(n,x)`:
```
def build_exp_model(n, x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    x=np.clip(x, -8.0, 8.0)
    t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    u=t-0.5
    logf = x[0]*u + x[1]*u*u + x[2]*u*u*u
    # sinusoids centered at 0.5
    for k in range(3):
       idx=3+2*k
       A=x[idx]; B=x[idx+1]
       ang = 2.0*np.pi*(k)*t  # or k*t? centered? Use angle = 2π k (t) maybe phase arbitrary.
       # maybe include lobe: ang = 2π k * u? since k=1, centered.
       logf += A*np.sin(ang)+ B*np.cos(ang)
    # allow an optional damping endpoint? Could include x3*(1-u^2) etc? but sinusoids enough.
    maxlog=np.max(logf)
    # subtract max to avoid overflow
    logf -= maxlog
    # dynamic range
    logf = np.clip(logf, -25.0, 0.0)
    f=np.exp(logf)
    return normalize_to_sum(f)
```
Need maybe x have length 9. Could include linear endpoint term `x[3]*t` and quadratic `x[4]*t*t` instead of centered to better endpoint. Let's use 6 polynomial + 6 trig? Keep m=9:
0: linear t
1: quadratic (t-0.5)^2
2: cubic (t-0.5)^3
3-4: sin/cos 1
5-6: sin/cos 2
7-8: sin/cos 3
We can add x9: quadratic on t (not centered?) center okay. Use t not centered for linear? Fine.
Maybe add shape endpoint double: x[9]* ( (t-0.25)^2? )
No.

Custom parameter local search:
```
def coordinate_param_search(x, n, deadline, rng):
    best_x=x; best_f=param_objective(best_x,n)
    scales = np.array([...])
    no_improve=0
    while time<deadline:
        d = rng.integers(m)
        step = rng.choice([-1,1]) * scales[d] * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.7))
        # adaptive scale? Maintain step sizes? Simpler: try a few step sizes geometric.
        x[d]=best_x[d]+step
        f=param_objective(x,n)
        if improves: best_x=x; best_f=f; no_improve=0
        else: x[d]=best_x[d]
        # occasional jump random dimension all
        if no_improve > 50: best_x += rng.normal(0,0.3,m); ...
```

Full-vector local search `improve_sequence`:
Input `a` normalized. Use bottleneck. We need avoid too many active update candidates if too slow. For n=8192, each candidate `new_a` log operations O(n), FFT O(n log n). Active gradient compute O(T*n). Python loops over top lags, vector slices; T maybe 5. 1ms? Fine. We can do thousands.

Implement:
```
def improve_active(a, deadline, rng, eval_every=1):
    n=len(a); S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=normalize_to_sum(a,S)
    best=a; best_score=internal_score(a)
    C=fft_convolution(a); nfft? not needed
    no_improve=0
    lr_candidates=[0.003,0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3]
    top_base = 3
    it=0
    while time<deadline:
       it+=1
       maxC=np.max(C)
       if maxC<=0: break
       # choose active lags: top indices sorted by C
       # We can recompute top each iteration using argsort? O(n log n) too expensive. Instead random sample from high C.
       # Use list of top via np.argpartition each iteration? 16k log 16k ~ fine maybe 0.5ms.
       if it % 10 ==0 or active_lags is None:
           top_idx = np.argpartition(C, -8)[-8:]
           active_lags = top_idx[np.argsort(C[top_idx])[::-1]][:3]
       # else perturbed active: include current top plus random high?
       # Candidate 1: log update for each lr
       for lr in lr_candidates:
           cand=active_log_cand(best, active_lags, lr, rng)
           sc=internal_score(cand)
           if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
               best=cand; best_score=sc; C=fft_convolution(cand); no_improve=0; break
       else:
           no_improve+=1
           # if stuck, random perturb block, spectral, pair
           if no_improve % 20 ==0:
              cand=block_perturb(best, rng, n); sc=...
           if no_improve > 500:
              # reset best to current? Or restart from best with larger lr
```
But if each `internal_score` recomputes FFT, active_log_cand could also need C? It computes new a only. Scoring recomputes C. Active gradient uses old C? We have C. For top lags, contributions vector can be computed from best. Good.

`active_log_cand(a, active_lags, lr, rng, noise=0.0)`:
```
def active_log_cand(a, active_lags, lr, rng=None, noise=0.0):
    n=len(a)
    loga=np.log(a+1e-300)
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    for k in active_lags:
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
       if hi>=lo:
           # complement slice for k-i, i from lo..hi
           c = a[lo:hi+1] * a[k-hi : k-lo+1][::-1]
           grad[lo:hi+1] += c
    gmax=np.max(grad)
    if gmax>0:
       loga -= lr * grad / gmax
    if noise>0 and rng is not None:
       # add smoothed noise: simple random but blur by averaging
       noise_arr = rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
       # maybe blur by adding to neighbors: simple convolution with [0.25,0.5,0.25]
       if n>3:
          noise_arr = 0.25*noise_arr[:-2] + 0.5*noise_arr + 0.25*noise_arr[2:]
       loga += noise_arr
    logf = loga - np.max(loga)
    logf = np.clip(logf, -25.0, 0.0)
    return normalize_to_sum(np.exp(logf), np.sqrt(2*n))
```
But subtract max and dynamic range every update may change shape drastically. Since a normalized has log values not centered; subtract max makes max log 0 and others negative; dynamic range clip to 25. If original min already < -25, clip to 0; okay. This also resets multiplicative constant; normalize later. Good.

Potential issue: `loga` values from a with sum S: a max ~100, log~4.6; subtract max leaves values -25..0. Exp then normalize by sum ~? Sum of exp maybe ~1 if one max; multiply by S gives max ~S. That's okay. But doing this every update naturally keeps dynamic range ≤25. If original shape has max/min ratio > e25, clipped. Fine.

Maybe adding noise to loga with blur helps. Use noise scale 0.02.

`block_perturb`:
```
def block_perturb(a, rng):
   n=len(a); a=np.array(a)
   width=int(rng.integers(max(8,n//64), max(16,n//8)))
   start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
   a[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.3))
   # occasional invert block? maybe
   return normalize_to_sum(a)
```
Since values positive.

`spectral_perturb`:

```
def spectral_perturb(a, rng, mode):
   loga=np.log(a+eps)
   F=np.fft.rfft(loga)
   freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
   if mode==0: F *= 1/np.sqrt(1+(freq*sigma_view)**2)
   else: random phase? maybe
   new=irfft
```

Pair transfer function maybe:
```
def pair_transfer_cand(a, active_lags, rng, frac):
   a=a.copy(); C? no use active
   for k in active_lags:
      lo... contrib
      if sum>0:
         source = weighted choice contrib / sum
         # destination: choose with low contrib among all valid for that k? To minimize active k
         weight_dest = 1.0/(contrib+1e-15) maybe valid all? Or uniform over n.
         dest = choice
         delta=frac*a[source]
         if delta>0 and a[source]>=delta:
             a[source]-=delta; a[dest]+=delta
   return normalize_to_sum(a)
```

```
def resample_sequence(a, n):
   m=len(a)
   if m==n: return a
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
   return normalize(b)
```

Pseudo:
```
def improve_candidate(a, deadline, rng, max_evals=20000):
   best=normalize(a); best_score=score(best); C=conv(best)
   curr=best.copy(); curr_score=best_score
   no_improve=0; evals=0
   while time<deadline and evals<max_evals:
      # active lags from C of curr? use current score C.
      active = top_lags(C, k=3)
      # generate several moves on curr; if improve best, update best; if improve curr but not best, accept (annealing?)
      generated=0
      for lr in [0.005,0.02,0.08]:
          cand=active_log_cand(curr,active,lr,rng,noise=0.01)
          score=score(cand)
          if score < curr_score - 1e-12: # accept hill climb
              curr=cand; curr_score=score; C=conv(curr); if score<best_score: best...
              no_improve=0; break
          elif score < best_score? should accept? if not improve curr but less than best? best >=curr. Can't.
      if no_improve>200:
         # random jump: curr = block_perturb(best, sigma slightly larger) or add noise
         curr=block_perturb(best,rng,large=True); curr_score=score(curr); C=conv(curr); no_improve=0
      if no_improve>2000:
         # reset curr to best and continue maybe larger lr
         curr=best.copy(); curr_score=best_score; C=conv(curr)
      evals += maybe count each score
   return best,best_score
```

def add_blurred_noise(loga, rng, sigma):
    noise=rng.normal(0, sigma, len(loga))
    # kernel [0.25,0.5,0.25]
    if n>2:
       out=np.empty_like(noise); out[1:-1]=0.25*noise[:-2]+0.5*noise[1:-1]+0.25*noise[2:]; edges copy
    return loga+out
```

Block perturb:
```
def block_perturb(a, rng, sigma=0.2, nblocks=1):
   n=len(a); cand=a.copy()
   for _ in range(nblocks):
      frac=np.exp(rng.normal(np.log(0.1),0.5)); width=max(4,int(n*frac))
      start=rng.integers(0,n-width+1)
      cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma))
   return normalize_to_sum(cand)
```
 `normalize_to_sum` target sqrt(2n). Good.

Multiple starts generation top-level function:
```
def make_param_starts(rng, m, fixed=True):
   starts=[]
   starts.append(np.zeros(m))
   # exponential tilts
   for p in [-3,-2,-1,1,2,3,4]:
      x=np.zeros(m); x[0]=p; starts.append(x)
   # quadratic peaks
   for q in [1,2,4,8]: x[1]=q
   # sinus
   for amp in [0.5,1,1.5]: x[3]=amp or x[4]
   # random 8
   for _ in range(8): starts.append(rng.normal(0, [1,1,0.5,1,1,0.7,0.7,0.5,0.5]))
   return starts
```

def optimize_param_set(n, starts, deadline, rng):
   best_x=None; best_f=inf
   scale_eps = 1e-4
   for x0 in starts:
      if time.expired: break
      # quick local param coordinate? Use scipy
      res=None
      try:
         res=opt.minimize(param_objective, x0, args=(n,), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':500,'fatol':1e-6,'xatol':1e-4})
      except: pass
      x=res.x if res and finite else x0
      f=param_objective(x,n)
      if f<best_f: best_f... best_x
      # Also run a second minimize from res with slightly larger steps? Maybe.
   return best_x, best_f
```

Custom param search:
```
def coordinate_param_search(x0, n, deadline, rng, max_iter=5000):
   m=len(x0)
   x=x0.copy(); f=param_objective(x,n)
   steps = np.array([1.0,0.3,0.1,0.5,0.5,0.3,0.3,0.2,0.2])*? Maybe initial 0.5.
   best_x=x.copy(); best_f=f
   for it in range(max_iter):
       if time>=deadline: break
       d=int(rng.integers(m))
       # choose step size multiplier lognormal
       mult=np.exp(rng.normal(0,1.0))
       direction=1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1
       old=x[d]
       x[d]=np.clip(old + direction*steps[d]*mult, -8.0,8.0)
       f_new=param_objective(x,n)
       if f_new < f:
           f=f_new
           if f < best_f: best_f=f; best_x=x.copy()
           # increase step slightly
           steps[d]=min(4.0, steps[d]*1.2)
       else:
           x[d]=old
           # decrease step if many failing? Keep no_improve count per dimension
       # occasional random restart in some dims
   return best_x,best_f
```

def lp_candidate(m, seed, deadline, rng):
   S=np.sqrt(2*m)
   a = build random/exponential normalized to m
   f = a
   M = max(f*f) via FFT
   for it in range until deadline:
      # build A dense rows maybe if m<=512
      A = np.zeros((2*m-1,m), dtype=float)
      for k in range(2*m-1): fill
      # normalize b = M
      res = linprog(c=-np.ones(m), A_ub=A, b_ub=M, bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
      if res.success:
          g=res.x
          gsum=g.sum()
          if gsum>0:
             g = normalize_to_sum(g, S)
             sc=internal_score(g)
             if sc < current: current=g; M=max...
      else break
   return best
```

Resampling function:
```
def resample_sequence(a, n):
    m=len(a); if m==n: return a
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_to_sum(b)
```
This may smooth; local refine can adjust.

Let's design `refine_params` with a step list initialized based on param magnitude. We'll call after each minimize. It is top-level and uses `param_objective`.

```
def refine_params(x, n, deadline, rng):
    m=len(x)
    x=x.copy(); f=param_objective(x,n)
    steps=np.array([1.0,0.5,0.2,1.0,1.0,0.5,0.5,0.3,0.3])
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(3000):
       if time.expired break
       d=rng.integers(m)
       old=x[d]
       # propose 3 step sizes maybe
       improved=False
       for mult in (1.0,0.5,2.0):
           delta = rng.choice([-1,1])*steps[d]*mult
           newv=old+delta
           if abs(newv)>8: newv=np.sign(newv)*8
           x[d]=newv
           fn=param_objective(x,n)
           if fn < f - 1e-10:
              f=fn; improved=True; steps[d]=min(4, steps[d]*1.2); break
           # if not, revert at end
       if improved: no_improve=0
       else:
          x[d]=old
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve > 100:
             # jump
             x[d]=old+rng.normal(0,1)
             fn=param_objective(x,n)
             if fn<f: f=fn; no_improve=0
             else x[d]=old; steps[d]=max(0.05, steps[d]*0.7)
    return x,f
```
This does up to 3 evals per iteration; okay. Use deadline.

But param_objective called many; if n large, still okay.

Maybe use coordinate descent over lags: Choose source and destination based on active contributions; not just log uniform. The log update reduces all contributors equally in log. If max lag has a few large contributors, maybe need targeted. We can implement `targeted_log_cand`:

Let's design `targeted_block_cand(a, active_lags, rng)`:
For each active k:
  contrib vector length valid. Source indices = where contrib > high quantile; dest indices = where contrib < low quantile. Multiply all sources by (1-delta), dest by (1+delta) or random subset. Usually active lags overlap; could do one k.
Implementation simple:
```
source_mask = contrib > np.percentile(contrib, 80)
dest_mask = contrib < np.percentile(contrib, 20)
cand = a.copy(); cand[source_mask] *= np.exp(-lr); cand[dest_mask]*=np.exp(lr)
normalize
```
This can focus. But modifying all high contributors may reduce mass too much; normalize.

def improve_sequence(a, deadline, rng, max_iters=200000):
    a=normalize_to_sum(a)
    n=len(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=internal_score(a); C=fft_convolution(a)
    curr=a.copy(); curr_score=best_score; curr_C=C
    no_improve=0; eval_count=0
    top_k=4
    # Precompute lrs
    lrs=(0.004,0.012,0.035,0.1)
    # active update candidate function inline? Use call.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and eval_count < max_iters:
       # get active lags from curr_C
       if curr_C is None: curr_C=fft_convolution(curr)
       L=len(curr_C)
       top=len(curr_C)
       if top > top_k:
          idxp=np.argpartition(curr_C, -top_k)[-top_k:]
          # if duplicates ok
          # sort by value
          idxp=idxp[np.argsort(curr_C[idxp])[::-1]]
       else: idxp=np.arange(L)
       active=idxp[:3]
       improved=False
       # active log lrs
       for lr in lrs:
           cand=active_log_cand(curr, active, lr, rng, noise=0.008)
           sc=internal_score(cand); eval_count+=1
           if sc < curr_score - 1e-10:
               curr=cand; curr_C=fft_convolution(cand); curr_score=sc; improved=True
               if sc < best_score: best=curr.copy(); best_score=sc
               break
       if not improved:
           # try targeted block for one active lag
           cand=targeted_cand(curr, active[0], rng)
           sc=internal_score(cand); eval_count+=1
           ... accept if improves curr
           if not improved:
               # periodic block perturb or anneal
               if no_improve % 30 == 0:
                   cand=block_perturb(curr,rng,sigma=0.05+0.1*(no_improve/1000))
                   sc=...
                   # accept if improves curr or best? Could accept worsening with probability? Use simulated: if sc < curr_score + 0.01 accept
                   if sc < curr_score + 0.01:
                       curr=cand; curr_C=...; curr_score=sc
                       if sc<best_score: best...
                       no_improve=0
                   else: no_improve+=1
               else:
                   no_improve+=1
       if no_improve > 300:
           # reset current to best but with perturbation to escape
           curr=block_perturb(best,rng,sigma=0.2)
           curr_score=internal_score(curr); curr_C=fft_convolution(curr); eval_count+=1
           no_improve=0
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
    return best,best_score

```
def targeted_cand(a,k,rng):
   n=len(a); lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
   if hi<lo: return a
   contrib=a[lo:hi+1]*a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
   total=contrib.sum()
   if total<=0: return a
   cand=a.copy()
   # decreasing high contributors: choose top 25%
   p80=np.percentile(contrib,80)
   p20=np.percentile(contrib,20)
   if p80>0:
      high=(contrib >= p80)
      low=(contrib <= p20)
      # ensure some high and low
      hr=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.12))
      cand[lo+np.where(high)[0]] *= np.exp(-hr)
      cand[lo+np.where(low)[0]] *= np.exp(hr)
   # occasionally transfer one weighted
   return normalize_to_sum(cand)
```

def resample_sequence(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.nan_to_num...; if len==0 return ones
   m=len(a)
   if m==n: return normalize_to_sum(a)
   # np.interp expects increasing x; if a all zeros? ok.
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
   return normalize_to_sum(b)

def active_log_cand(a, active_lags, lr, rng, noise=0.0):
    n=a.size
    loga=np.log(a, where=a>0, out=np.full(n, -25.0), initial?)
```

```
    grad=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
    max_c=0.0
    for k in active_lags:
        k=int(k)
        lo = 0 if k < n-1? Actually lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
        if hi>=lo:
           comp = a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
           contrib = a[lo:hi+1] * comp
           grad[lo:hi+1] += contrib
    gmax=grad.max()
    if gmax > 1e-300:
       loga -= lr * grad / gmax
    if noise >0 and rng is not None:
       noise_arr = rng.normal(0, noise, n)
       if n > 3:
          # simple blur with kernel [0.25,0.5,0.25]
          blurred = np.empty_like(noise_arr)
          blurred[1:-1] = 0.25*noise_arr[:-2] + 0.5*noise_arr[1:-1] + 0.25*noise_arr[2:]
          blurred[0] = 0.75*noise_arr[0] + 0.25*noise_arr[1]
          blurred[-1] = 0.25*noise_arr[-2] + 0.75*noise_arr[-1]
       else: blurred=noise_arr
       loga += blurred
    logf = loga - loga.max()
    logf = np.clip(logf, -25.0, 0.0)
    return normalize_to_sum(np.exp(logf))
```

`block_perturb`:
```
def block_perturb(a, rng, sigma=0.1, min_width=8):
   n=a.size
   cand=a.copy()
   width=int(rng.integers(max(min_width, n//64), max(min_width+1, n//6)))
   start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
   cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma))
   # occasionally second block
   if rng.random()<0.3:
      width2=int(rng.integers(max(4,n//256), max(5,n//32)))
      start2=int(rng.integers(0,n-width2+1))
      cand[start2:start2+width2] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma*0.7))
   return normalize_to_sum(cand)
```

Let's write `refine_params` carefully. It needs `deadline` and `rng`. We'll use `param_objective` and `B`. Step sizes scale with parameter.
```
def refine_params(x, B, deadline, rng, max_iters=2000):
    m=x.size
    x=x.copy()
    f=param_objective(x,B)
    steps=np.array([1.0,0.5,0.2,1.0,1.0,0.5,0.5,0.3,0.3], dtype=float)
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
        d=int(rng.integers(m))
        old=x[d]
        improved=False
        # try 3 magnitudes
        base=steps[d]
        for mult in (1.0,0.5,2.0):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
            delta = (1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0) * base * mult
            newv=old+delta
            if newv>8: newv=8
            elif newv<-8: newv=-8
            x[d]=newv
            fn=param_objective(x,B)
            if fn < f - 1e-10:
                f=fn; improved=True; steps[d]=min(5.0, steps[d]*1.1)
                break
        if improved:
            no_improve=0
        else:
            x[d]=old
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 50:
                # random jump in one or two dims
                for _ in range(2):
                    d2=int(rng.integers(m))
                    old2=x[d2]
                    newv=old2 + rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
                    newv=clip
                    x[d2]=newv
                    fn=param_objective(x,B)
                    if fn < f:
                       f=fn; no_improve=0; x_kept? else revert? If one of two improves, keep; simpler revert if not.
                if not improved: steps[d]=max(0.05, steps[d]*0.8)
    return x,f
```

def make_param_starts(rng, m):
    starts=[]
    z=np.zeros(m)
    starts.append(z.copy())
    for v in (-4,-2,-1,1,2,4):
        x=z.copy(); if m>0: x[0]=v; starts.append(x)
    for v in (1,2,4,8):
        x=z.copy(); if m>1: x[1]=v; starts.append(x)
    # sin/cos fundamental
    for a,b in [(0,1),(1,0),(-1,0),(1,1),(-1,1)]:
        x=z.copy(); if m>3: x[3]=a
        if m>4: x[4]=b
        starts.append(x)
    # combinations with tilt
    for tilt,samp in [(1,1),(1,-1),(-1,1),(-1,-1)]:
        x=z.copy(); x[0]=tilt; x[3]=samp; starts.append(x)
    # random
    scales=np.array([2.0,1.0,0.5,1.0,0.7,0.5,0.5,0.3,0.3])
    for _ in range(8): starts.append(rng.normal(0,1,size=m)*scales)
    return starts
```

Now, possible bug in `normalize_to_sum`: If target_s None and a.size less than 1, returns ones? Input nonempty. For resample, n>0. Clip after scaling may change sum; values likely not clipped. But to be safe:
```
def normalize_to_sum(a, target_s=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        a=np.ones(a.size)
        s=a.size
    if target_s is None:
        target_s=np.sqrt(2.0*a.size)
    a *= (target_s / s)
    a=np.minimum(a,1000.0)
    # if clipping reduced sum, rescale if possible
    s2=a.sum()
    if s2 > 0 and abs(target_s - s2) > 1e-6*target_s:
        a *= target_s/s2
        a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    return a
```
If second scale exceeds1000? values small. Good.

`fft_convolution`:
```
def fft_convolution(a):
    n = len(a)
    L = 2*n - 1
    nfft=1
    while nfft < L: nfft <<= 1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=nfft)
    if c.size > L: c=c[:L]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
`fa*fa` complex squared, not magnitude squared. Good. For real convolution, use square, not conjugate. `fa*fa` (not fa*np.conj(fa)). Important! For autocorrelation would be |fa|^2. We need square. Since `fa` complex, `fa*fa` correct. But if using `rfft` returns complex half; square okay. Use `fa * fa` not `fa ** 2` same.

Check with a=[1,2]: nfft=4, fa DFT; ifft(fa^2) first 3 = [1,4,4]. Yes.

```
def improve_sequence(a, deadline, rng):
    a=normalize_to_sum(a)
    n=a.size
    best=a.copy(); best_score,_=score_and_conv(a)
    curr=a.copy(); curr_score=best_score; curr_C=None
    print? maybe not inside to avoid too much? We can print start/end if verbosity? Not necessary.
    no_improve=0
    lrs=(0.004,0.012,0.035,0.09)
    top_count=4
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it +=1
        if curr_C is None or it % 50 == 0:
            curr_C = fft_convolution(curr)
        # active
        Lc=curr_C.size
        if Lc <= top_count:
            active = np.argsort(curr_C)[-top_count:][::-1]
        else:
            part = np.argpartition(curr_C, -top_count)[-top_count:]
            active = part[np.argsort(curr_C[part])[::-1]]
        active = active[:3]
        improved=False
        for lr in lrs:
            cand=active_log_cand(curr, active, lr, rng, 0.006)
            sc, cnd = score_and_conv(cand)
            if sc < curr_score - 1e-10:
                curr=cand; curr_C=cnd; curr_score=sc; improved=True
                if sc < best_score:
                    best=curr.copy(); best_score=sc
                break
        if not improved:
            cand=targeted_cand(curr, active[0], rng)
            sc,cnd=score_and_conv(cand)
            if sc < curr_score - 1e-10:
               ...
               improved=True
        if not improved:
            no_improve +=1
            if no_imprime % 20 == 0:
                cand=block_perturb(curr, rng, sigma=0.03+0.01*min(5.0,no_improve/100))
                sc,cnd=score_and_conv(cand)
                # accept if small improvement or small worsening with probability
                if sc < curr_score + 0.01:
                    curr=cand; curr_C=cnd; curr_score=sc
                    if sc < best_score: best=curr.copy(); best_score=sc
                    no_improve=0
            if no_improve > 250:
                # reset current to best with moderate perturbation
                if rng.random() < 0.7:
                    cand=block_perturb(best, rng, sigma=0.25)
                else:
                    cand=active_log_cand(best, active, 0.2, rng, noise=0.2)
                sc,cnd=score_and_conv(cand)
                curr=cand; curr_C=cnd; curr_score=sc
                no_improve=0
        # if no improvement for very long, try more aggressive noise? no
    return best,best_score
```

Define top-level `top_active_lags(C, k=3)`:
```
def top_active_lags(C,k=3):
   m=C.size
   if m<=k: return np.argsort(C)[::-1]
   part=np.argpartition(C,-k)[-k:]
   return part[np.argsort(C[part])[::-1]]
```

```python
"""Search low-peak self-convolution nonnegative step sequences.

The program combines:
  * a smooth exponential/trigonometric parameterization,
  * derivative-free parameter exploitation,
  * full-vector active-coefficient log-domain local search,
  * occasional coarse-resolution local search and resampling,
  * final verification with the supplied evaluate_sequence().

All sequences are kept nonnegative and normalized to a moderate scale.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _sci_opt
except Exception:
    _sci_opt = None


PARAM_M = 9
LOG_DYNAMIC_RANGE = 25.0
PARAM_CLIP = 8.0


def choose_primary_n(h):
    if h is not None and h.size >= 1024 and h.size <= 20000:
        return int(h.size)
    return 8192


def load_height_sequence():
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
            arr = np.asarray(globals()["height_sequence_1"], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            if arr.size > 0:
                return arr
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def make_param_basis(n):
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    u = t - 0.5
    u2 = u * u
    u3 = u2 * u

    ang1 = 2.0 * np.pi * u
    ang2 = 4.0 * np.pi * u
    ang3 = 6.0 * np.pi * u

    parts = [
        u,
        u2,
        u3,
        np.sin(ang1),
        np.cos(ang1),
        np.sin(ang2),
        np.cos(ang2),
        np.sin(ang3),
        np.cos(ang3),
    ]
    return np.column_stack(parts).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def normalize_to_sum(a, target_s=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)

    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)

    if target_s is None:
        target_s = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(a.size))

    a = a * (float(target_s) / s)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)

    s2 = float(np.sum(a))
    if s2 > 0.0 and abs(s2 - float(target_s)) > 1e-8 * max(1.0, abs(float(target_s))):
        a = a * (float(target_s) / s2)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    return a


def fft_convolution(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    length = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = 1
    while nfft < length:
        nfft <<= 1

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=nfft)
    if c.size > length:
        c = c[:length]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def score_and_conv(a):
    c = fft_convolution(a)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf"), c
    val = float(2.0 * a.size * float(np.max(c)) / (s * s))
    if not math.isfinite(val):
        return float("inf"), c
    return val, c


def internal_score(a):
    val, _ = score_and_conv(a)
    return val


def build_from_basis(B, x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.clip(x, -PARAM_CLIP, PARAM_CLIP)

    logf = B.dot(x)
    logf = logf - np.max(logf)
    logf = np.clip(logf, -LOG_DYNAMIC_RANGE, 0.0)
    f = np.exp(logf)
    return normalize_to_sum(f)


def param_objective(x, B):
    a = build_from_basis(B, x)
    val = internal_score(a)
    if not math.isfinite(val):
        return 1.0e9
    return float(val)


def make_param_starts(rng):
    starts = []
    z = np.zeros(PARAM_M, dtype=np.float64)

    # Promising one-parameter shapes first.
    for v in (2.0, -2.0, 1.0, -1.0, 3.0, -3.0, 0.5, -0.5):
        x = z.copy()
        x[0] = v
        starts.append(x)

    for v in (1.0, 2.0, 4.0, -1.0, -2.0):
        x = z.copy()
        x[1] = v
        starts.append(x)

    # Single low harmonics, including phase choices.
    for s1, c1 in ((0.0, 1.0), (1.0, 0.0), (-1.0, 0.0), (1.0, 1.0), (-1.0, 1.0), (0.8, 0.3)):
        x = z.copy()
        x[3] = s1
        x[4] = c1
        starts.append(x)

    # Mix of tilt and fundamental modulation.
    for t1, s1 in ((1.0, 0.8), (-1.0, 0.8), (1.0, -0.8), (-1.0, -0.8), (1.5, 1.2), (-1.5, -1.2)):
        x = z.copy()
        x[0] = t1
        x[3] = s1
        starts.append(x)

    # More harmonics.
    for s1, c1, s2, c2 in (
        (0.8, 0.2, 0.5, 0.1),
        (-0.8, 0.2, -0.5, 0.1),
        (1.0, -0.2, 0.6, 0.2),
        (-1.0, -0.2, -0.6, 0.2),
    ):
        x = z.copy()
        x[3] = s1
        x[4] = c1
        x[5] = s2
        x[6] = c2
        starts.append(x)

    # Uniform fallback.
    starts.append(z.copy())

    # Random starts.
    scales = np.array([2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.5, 0.3, 0.3], dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(12):
        starts.append(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=PARAM_M) * scales)

    return starts


def refine_params(x, B, deadline, rng):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    m = x.size
    if m <= 0:
        return x, param_objective(x, B)

    f = param_objective(x, B)

    steps = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) * 0.7
    if m > 0:
        steps[0] = 1.0
    if m > 1:
        steps[1] = 0.5
    if m > 2:
        steps[2] = 0.25
    if m > 3:
        steps[3] = 1.0
    if m > 4:
        steps[4] = 0.7
    if m > 5:
        steps[5] = 0.5
    if m > 6:
        steps[6] = 0.5
    if m > 7:
        steps[7] = 0.3
    if m > 8:
        steps[8] = 0.3

    no_improve = 0

    for _ in range(5000):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        d = int(rng.integers(m))
        old = x[d]
        improved = False

        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 2.0):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            direction = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
            newv = old + direction * steps[d] * mult
            if newv > PARAM_CLIP:
                newv = PARAM_CLIP
            elif newv < -PARAM_CLIP:
                newv = -PARAM_CLIP

            x[d] = newv
            fn = param_objective(x, B)

            if fn < f - 1e-11:
                f = fn
                improved = True
                steps[d] = min(5.0, steps[d] * 1.15)
                break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            x[d] = old
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 60:
                xj = x.copy()
                idx = rng.integers(m, size=2)
                xj[idx] += rng.normal(0.0, 1.7, size=2)
                xj = np.clip(xj, -PARAM_CLIP, PARAM_CLIP)
                fj = param_objective(xj, B)
                if fj < f:
                    x = xj
                    f = fj
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    steps[d] = max(0.05, steps[d] * 0.9)

    return x, float(f)


def optimize_parameters(n, deadline, rng, h_seq=None):
    B = make_param_basis(n)
    starts = make_param_starts(rng)

    # A least-squares fit to the supplied sequence can give a useful initialization.
    if h_seq is not None and h_seq.size == n:
        try:
            hp = h_seq / float(np.sum(h_seq))
            logh = np.log(hp + 1.0e-12)
            xs, _, _, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(B, logh, rcond=None)
            xs = np.clip(xs, -PARAM_CLIP, PARAM_CLIP)
            starts.insert(0, xs)
        except Exception:
            pass

    evaluated = []
    for idx, x0 in enumerate(starts):
        v = param_objective(x0, B)
        evaluated.append((float(v), idx, np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()))

    evaluated.sort()

    best_x = evaluated[0][2].copy()
    best_f = evaluated[0][0]

    top_count = 8 if n <= 8192 else 5
    maxfev = 2500 if n <= 4096 else (1600 if n <= 8192 else 900)

    for f0, idx, x0 in evaluated[:top_count]:
        if time.monotonic() >= max(time.monotonic(), deadline - 2.0):
            break

        x = x0.copy()
        f = f0

        if _sci_opt is not None:
            try:
                res = _sci_opt.minimize(
                    param_objective,
                    x,
                    args=(B,),
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={
                        "maxfev": maxfev,
                        "fatol": 1e-7,
                        "xatol": 1e-5,
                    },
                )
                if res is not None:
                    if hasattr(res, "fun") and res.fun is not None and math.isfinite(float(res.fun)):
                        if float(res.fun) < f:
                            f = float(res.fun)
                            if res.x is not None:
                                x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            except Exception:
                pass

        if f < best_f:
            best_f = float(f)
            best_x = x.copy()

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        short_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 60.0)
        best_x, best_f = refine_params(best_x, B, short_deadline, rng)

    best_a = build_from_basis(B, best_x)
    return best_a, best_x, float(best_f)


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return normalize_to_sum(np.ones(n))

    m = a.size
    if m == n:
        return normalize_to_sum(a)

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)

    xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(xnew, xold, a)
    return normalize_to_sum(b)


def to_probability(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones_like(a) * (1.0 / max(1, a.size))
    return a / s


def active_log_cand(a, active_lags, lr, rng, noise=0.0):
    n = int(a.size)
    loga = np.log(a + 1.0e-300)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    for k in active_lags:
        k = int(k)
        lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi < lo:
            continue
        contrib = a[lo:hi + 1] * (a[k - hi: k - lo + 1][::-1])
        grad[lo:hi + 1] += contrib

    gmax = float(np.max(grad)) if grad.size else 0.0
    if gmax > 1e-300:
        loga = loga - (float(lr) * grad / gmax)

    if noise > 0.0 and rng is not None:
        nz = rng.normal(0.0, float(noise), size=n)
        if n > 3:
            blurred = np.empty_like(nz)
            blurred[1:-1] = 0.25 * nz[:-2] + 0.5 * nz[1:-1] + 0.25 * nz[2:]
            blurred[0] = 0.75 * nz[0] + 0.25 * nz[1]
            blurred[-1] = 0.25 * nz[-2] + 0.75 * nz[-1]
            loga = loga + blurred
        else:
            loga = loga + nz

    loga = loga - np.max(loga)
    loga = np.clip(loga, -LOG_DYNAMIC_RANGE, 0.0)
    return normalize_to_sum(np.exp(loga))


def block_perturb(a, rng, sigma=0.1):
    n = int(a.size)
    cand = a.copy()

    low_width = max(4, n // 64)
    high_width = max(low_width + 1, n // 6)
    width = int(rng.integers(low_width, high_width))
    width = min(width, n)
    if width > 0:
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        cand[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))

    if rng.random() < 0.35:
        low2 = max(2, n // 256)
        high2 = max(low2 + 1, n // 32)
        w2 = int(rng.integers(low2, high2))
        w2 = min(w2, n)
        if w2 > 0:
            s2 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w2 + 1)))
            cand[s2:s2 + w2] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.7 * sigma)))

    return normalize_to_sum(cand)


def targeted_cand(a, k, rng):
    n = int(a.size)
    k = int(k)
    cand = a.copy()

    lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
    hi = min(n - 1, k)
    if hi < lo:
        return cand

    contrib = a[lo:hi + 1] * (a[k - hi: k - lo + 1][::-1])
    if contrib.size == 0:
        return cand

    cmax = float(np.max(contrib))
    cmin = float(np.min(contrib))
    if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= cmin * 1.001:
        return cand

    p75 = float(np.percentile(contrib, 75))
    p25 = float(np.percentile(contrib, 25))

    high = contrib >= p75
    low = contrib <= p25

    if high.any() and low.any():
        hr = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.14))
        seg = cand[lo:hi + 1]
        seg[high] *= float(np.exp(-hr))
        seg[low] *= float(np.exp(hr))

    # Occasionally perform one explicit weighted transfer.
    if rng.random() < 0.25:
        total = float(np.sum(contrib))
        if total > 0.0:
            probs = contrib / total
            inx = int(np.random.default_rng(rng.integers(1, 2**31 - 1)).choice(contrib.size, p=probs))
            src = lo + inx
            dst = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            delta = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25)) * a[src]
            if delta > 0.0 and a[src] >= delta:
                cand[src] -= delta
                cand[dst] += delta

    return normalize_to_sum(cand)


def top_active_lags(C, k=3):
    m = int(C.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    kk = max(1, int(k))
    if m <= kk:
        return np.argsort(C)[::-1]
    part = np.argpartition(C, -kk)[-kk:]
    return part[np.argsort(C[part])[::-1]]


def improve_sequence(a, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_to_sum(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score, _ = score_and_conv(a)

    curr = a.copy()
    curr_score = best_score
    curr_C = None
    active = np.array([], dtype=int)
    stale = True

    lrs = (0.005, 0.018, 0.055, 0.16)
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if stale:
            curr_C = fft_convolution(curr)
            active = top_active_lags(curr_C, k=4)[:3]
            stale = False

        if active.size == 0:
            break

        improved = False

        for lr in lrs:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            cand = active_log_cand(curr, active, lr, rng, noise=0.006)
            sc, cnd = score_and_conv(cand)
            if sc < curr_score - 1e-11:
                curr = cand
                curr_C = cnd
                curr_score = sc
                stale = True
                improved = True
                if sc < best_score:
                    best = curr.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                no_improve = 0
                break

        if not improved:
            if time.monotonic() < deadline:
                cand = targeted_cand(curr, active[0], rng)
                sc, cnd = score_and_conv(cand)
                if sc < curr_score - 1e-11:
                    curr = cand
                    curr_C = cnd
                    curr_score = sc
                    stale = True
                    improved = True
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best = curr.copy()
                        best_score = sc
                    no_improve = 0

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1

            if no_improve > 18 and time.monotonic() < deadline:
                sigma = 0.025 + 0.012 * min(8.0, no_improve / 80.0)
                cand = block_perturb(curr, rng, sigma=sigma)
                sc, cnd = score_and_conv(cand)
                # Permit small temporary worsening for exploration.
                if sc < curr_score + 0.012:
                    curr = cand
                    curr_C = cnd
                    curr_score = sc
                    stale = True
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best = curr.copy()
                        best_score = sc
                    no_improve = 0

            if no_improve > 260 and time.monotonic() < deadline:
                if rng.random() < 0.75:
                    cand = block_perturb(best, rng, sigma=0.28)
                else:
                    cand = active_log_cand(best, active, 0.22, rng, noise=0.25)
                sc, cnd = score_and_conv(cand)
                curr = cand
                curr_C = cnd
                curr_score = sc
                stale = True
                if sc < best_score:
                    best = curr.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                no_improve = 0

    return best, float(best_score)


def try_direct_evaluate(a):
    try:
        vals = [float(v) for v in np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
        sc = evaluate_sequence(vals)
        sc = float(sc)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            return sc
    except Exception:
        pass
    return float("inf")


def make_fallback(n=2048):
    a = np.ones(int(max(32, n)), dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_to_sum(a)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Return a nonnegative sequence minimizing the supplied score."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s if budget_s is not None else 2400.0)
    total = max(60.0, total)
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(30.0, total - 90.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    h = load_height_sequence()
    n_primary = choose_primary_n(h)
    target_s = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n_primary))

    print(f"[search] budget={total:.1f}s primary_n={n_primary} h_len={0 if h is None else h.size}", flush=True)

    h_primary = resample_sequence(h, n_primary) if h is not None else None
    if h_primary is not None:
        h_primary = normalize_to_sum(h_primary, target_s)

    candidates = []  # list of (internal_score, idx, name, array)
    cand_idx = 0

    def add_candidate(name, arr):
        nonlocal cand_idx
        try:
            arr = normalize_to_sum(arr)
            sc = internal_score(arr)
            if math.isfinite(sc):
                candidates.append((float(sc), cand_idx, name, arr))
                cand_idx += 1
        except Exception:
            pass

    # 1. Low-dimensional smooth parameter optimization.
    param_deadline = min(hard_deadline, t0 + min(180.0, 0.12 * total))
    try:
        param_seq, param_x, param_score = optimize_parameters(
            n_primary, param_deadline, rng, h_primary
        )
        print(f"[search] param internal={param_score:.6f} time={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}", flush=True)
        add_candidate("param_best", param_seq)

        # A few extra raw parameter samples for diversity.
        B_primary = make_param_basis(n_primary)
        scales = np.array([2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.5, 0.3, 0.3], dtype=np.float64)
        for _ in range(4):
            xr = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=PARAM_M) * scales
            add_candidate("param_rand", build_from_basis(B_primary, xr))
    except Exception as exc:
        print("[search] param opt failed:", type(exc).__name__, flush=True)
        param_seq = None

    # 2. Provided sequence, if useful.
    if h_primary is not None:
        add_candidate("provided", h_primary)

    if param_seq is not None and h_primary is not None:
        blend = to_probability(param_seq) * 0.5 + to_probability(h_primary) * 0.5
        add_candidate("blend_param_h", normalize_to_sum(blend, target_s))

    # 3. Uniform fallback so there is always something valid.
    add_candidate("uniform", np.ones(n_primary, dtype=np.float64))

    # 4. Coarse-resolution local search, then resample up.
    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 350.0:
        coarse_n = 1024 if n_primary >= 8192 else 512
        coarse_init = None
        if param_seq is not None:
            Bc = make_param_basis(coarse_n)
            coarse_init = build_from_basis(Bc, param_x)
        elif h_primary is not None:
            coarse_init = resample_sequence(h_primary, coarse_n)
        else:
            coarse_init = np.ones(coarse_n, dtype=np.float64)

        if coarse_init is not None:
            coarse_deadline = min(hard_deadline, time.monotonic() + 300.0)
            try:
                coarse_seq, coarse_score = improve_sequence(coarse_init, coarse_deadline, rng)
                print(f"[search] coarse internal={coarse_score:.6f} time={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}", flush=True)
                coarse_up = resample_sequence(coarse_seq, n_primary)
                add_candidate("coarse_upsample", coarse_up)
            except Exception as exc:
                print("[search] coarse failed:", type(exc).__name__, flush=True)

    # 5. Local refinement of the most promising candidates.
    if not candidates:
        return make_fallback(n_primary).tolist()

    evaluated_rank = [(float(sc), int(idx), name, np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).copy())
                      for (sc, idx, name, arr) in candidates]
    evaluated_rank.sort()

    remaining_alloc_end = max(time.monotonic() + 5.0, hard_deadline - 90.0)
    fractions = (0.38, 0.25, 0.15, 0.12, 0.10)

    for rank_pos in range(min(5, len(evaluated_rank))):
        if time.monotonic() >= remaining_alloc_end:
            break

        sc0, idx0, name0, arr0 = evaluated_rank[rank_pos]
        remaining = max(1.0, remaining_alloc_end - time.monotonic())
        frac = fractions[rank_pos] if rank_pos < len(fractions) else 0.05
        branch_time = max(15.0, remaining * frac)
        branch_deadline = min(remaining_alloc_end, time.monotonic() + branch_time)

        print(f"[local] branch {name0} internal={sc0:.6f} quota={branch_time:.1f}s", flush=True)

        try:
            improved_arr, improved_score = improve_sequence(arr0, branch_deadline, rng)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("[local] branch failed:", type(exc).__name__, flush=True)
            improved_arr, improved_score = arr0, sc0

        print(f"[local] branch {name0} improved internal={improved_score:.6f}", flush=True)

        # Replace this rank entry and re-sort for final selection.
        for i, entry in enumerate(evaluated_rank):
            if entry[1] == idx0:
                evaluated_rank[i] = (float(improved_score), idx0, name0 + "_refined",
                                     np.asarray(improved_arr, dtype=np.float64).copy())
                break

        evaluated_rank.sort()

    # Final direct verification of the best several candidates.
    best_arr = None
    best_direct = float("inf")
    evaluated_rank.sort()

    for pos in range(min(5, len(evaluated_rank))):
        if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 10.0:
            break

        sc0, idx0, name0, arr0 = evaluated_rank[pos]
        if arr0.size > 12000 and pos >= 2:
            break

        sc = try_direct_evaluate(arr0)
        print(f"[direct] {name0} score={sc:.6f} internal={sc0:.6f}", flush=True)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_direct:
            best_direct = sc
            best_arr = arr0.copy()

        if best_direct < 1.5027:
            # We already beat the stated target; still allow a tiny bit of polishing if time exists.
            if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 60.0:
                break

    if best_arr is None and evaluated_rank:
        best_arr = evaluated_rank[0][3].copy()

    if best_arr is None or best_arr.size == 0:
        best_arr = make_fallback(2048)

    best_arr = normalize_to_sum(best_arr)
    result = [float(v) for v in best_arr]

    try:
        final_sc = try_direct_evaluate(result)
        if math.isfinite(final_sc):
            print(f"[final] direct score={final_sc:.6f}", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"[final] direct score inf, internal fallback={internal_score(best_arr):.6f}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return result
```
